<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

## Original request

Every text block that contains ai generated text should have copy to clipboard and 'bro' buttons that appear when hovering over the text. Top right over the text. the bro button pumps the text through the 'bro' skill so that it's easier to read. bro acts as a toggle - when on it stays on and the button stays visible and blue. they should also have dictation buttons that starts taking dictation at the end of the text if there isn't already a carat
when hovering over text with a dictate button the dictation hotkeys should apply to the hovered text field, not the project chat
